#### KpOmråde

Områder for kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpGrense

Avgrensingsobjekt for områder for kommune(-del)plan (KpOmråde)

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpArealbrukOmråde

Områder med arealbruk i kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpBestemmelseOmråde

Område med bestemmelser til kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpBestemmelseGrense

Avgrensingsobjekt for områder med bestemmelser til kommune(-del)plan (KpBestemmelseOmråde)

#### KpFareGrense

Avgrensingsobjekt for områder for hensynssone, fare (KpFareSone)

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpStøyGrense

Avgrensingsobjekt for områder for hensynssone, støy (KpStøySone)

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpSamferdselLinje

Linjer som viktige ledd i kommunikasjonssystemet i kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

Områder for kommune(-del)plan

#### KpArealformålOmråde (flat)

Områder med arealformål i kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpInfrastrukturLinje (flat)

Linje for hensynssone med særlige krav til infrastruktur i kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpInfrastrukturGrense (flat)

Avgrensingsobjekt for områder for hensynssone, infrastruktur (KpInfrastrukturSone)

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>

#### KpPåskrift (flat)

Supplerende tekster til kommune(-del)plan

Geometri:<br />Elementtype: feature<br />Type: geometry-any<br />Lagrings-CRS:<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />Koordinatreferansesystem (crs):<br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/OGC/1.3/CRS84></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25832></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25833></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/25835></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4258></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/4326></a><br />• <a href="http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857"><http://www.opengis.net/def/crs/EPSG/0/3857></a>
